Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the arrays structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 569. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 796 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to arrays structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 569/796 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the arrays framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 840. The resulting operator achieves bound O(203/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 840 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the arrays structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 203. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
